│ I’m providing a clean, drop-in patch that adds true per-file integrity       │
│ verification and a safe staging flow. You can paste these scripts into your  │
│ /app directory.                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ What you’ll get                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  • /app/compress.py                                                          │
│     • Computes a SHA-256 for every input file                                │
│     • Writes sha256 into each shard’s manifest.json entry                    │
│     • Each shard contains at most 30 files                                   │
│     • Output manifests follow this shape: { "base_dir": "<parent of          │
│       input_dir>", "entries": [ { "internal_name": "...", "original_path":   │
│       "...", "size": ..., "sha256": "..." }, ... ] }                         │
│  • /app/decompress.py                                                        │
│     • Supports:                                                              │
│        • --stage-dir <path> to reconstruct into a staging tree first         │
│        • --verify to verify per-file sha256 against manifest                 │
│     • If staging is used, verifies staged files first and only then moves to │
│       final in-place paths                                                   │
│     • Verification focuses only on the original target tree (e.g.,           │
│       /app/c4_sample), not shard artifacts                                   │
│     • Prints a concise summary: Reconstructed X files, Y mismatches (if      │
│       any), plus a few path/hash samples                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ Code: /app/compress.py                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  #!/usr/bin/env python3                                                      │
│  import argparse                                                             │
│  import os                                                                   │
│  import json                                                                 │
│  import shutil                                                               │
│  from pathlib import Path                                                    │
│  import hashlib                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  MAX_FILES_PER_SHARD = 30                                                    │
│  MAX_FILE_SIZE = 15 * 1024 * 1024  # 15 MB                                   │
│                                                                              │
│  def sha256_for_path(path: Path, block_size: int = 65536) -&gt; str:         │
│      h = hashlib.sha256()                                                    │
│      with path.open(&#x27;rb&#x27;) as f:                                    │
│          for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(block_size), b&#x27;&#x27;):       │
│              h.update(chunk)                                                 │
│      return h.hexdigest()                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  def gather_files(input_root: Path):                                         │
│      for root, dirs, files in os.walk(input_root, topdown=True,              │
│  followlinks=False):                                                         │
│          root_path = Path(root)                                              │
│          # sort to ensure deterministic order                                │
│          for f in sorted(files):                                             │
│              fp = root_path / f                                              │
│              if fp.is_file():                                                │
│                  yield fp                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  def compress(input_dir: Path, output_dir: Path):                            │
│      input_dir = input_dir.resolve()                                         │
│      output_dir = output_dir.resolve()                                       │
│      if not input_dir.exists() or not input_dir.is_dir():                    │
│          raise SystemExit(f&quot;Input dir does not exist or is not a        │
│  directory: {input_dir}&quot;)                                               │
│      output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)                           │
│                                                                              │
│      input_root_parent = input_dir.parent                                    │
│                                                                              │
│      shard_index = 0                                                         │
│      items_in_shard = 0                                                      │
│      manifest_entries = []                                                   │
│      shard_dir = output_dir / f&quot;shard_{shard_index:04d}&quot;           │
│      shard_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)                            │
│      internal_counter = 0                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│      for file_path in gather_files(input_dir):                               │
│          size = file_path.stat().st_size                                     │
│          if size &gt; MAX_FILE_SIZE:                                         │
│              raise ValueError(f&quot;Input file too large for constraint     │
│  (15MB): {file_path} ({size} bytes)&quot;)                                   │
│                                                                              │
│          if items_in_shard &gt;= MAX_FILES_PER_SHARD:                        │
│              # finalize current shard manifest                               │
│              manifest_path = shard_dir / &#x27;manifest.json&#x27;           │
│              with manifest_path.open(&#x27;w&#x27;,                          │
│  encoding=&#x27;utf-8&#x27;) as mf:                                          │
│                  json.dump({&quot;base_dir&quot;: str(input_root_parent),    │
│  &quot;entries&quot;: manifest_entries}, mf, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)   │
│              # start new shard                                               │
│              shard_index += 1                                                │
│              shard_dir = output_dir / f&quot;shard_{shard_index:04d}&quot;   │
│              shard_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)                    │
│              manifest_entries = []                                           │
│              items_in_shard = 0                                              │
│              internal_counter = 0                                            │
│                                                                              │
│          internal_name = f&quot;data_{internal_counter:06d}.bin&quot;        │
│          dest_path = shard_dir / internal_name                               │
│          shutil.copy2(str(file_path), str(dest_path))                        │
│                                                                              │
│          original_rel = os.path.relpath(str(file_path),                      │
│  str(input_root_parent))                                                     │
│          sha256 = sha256_for_path(file_path)                                 │
│          manifest_entries.append({                                           │
│              &#x27;internal_name&#x27;: internal_name,                       │
│              &#x27;original_path&#x27;: original_rel,                        │
│              &#x27;size&#x27;: size,                                         │
│              &#x27;sha256&#x27;: sha256                                      │
│          })                                                                  │
│          items_in_shard += 1                                                 │
│          internal_counter += 1                                               │
│                                                                              │
│      if manifest_entries:                                                    │
│          manifest_path = shard_dir / &#x27;manifest.json&#x27;               │
│          with manifest_path.open(&#x27;w&#x27;, encoding=&#x27;utf-8&#x27;)  │
│  as mf:                                                                      │
│              json.dump({&quot;base_dir&quot;: str(input_root_parent),        │
│  &quot;entries&quot;: manifest_entries}, mf, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)   │
│                                                                              │
│      print(f&quot;Compression complete. Shards created under:                │
│  {output_dir}&quot;)                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│  def main():                                                                 │
│      parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=&#x27;Reshard data by      │
│  distributing files into shard directories.&#x27;)                           │
│      parser.add_argument(&#x27;input_dir&#x27;, help=&#x27;Input directory   │
│  containing files to reshard&#x27;)                                          │
│      parser.add_argument(&#x27;output_dir&#x27;, help=&#x27;Output           │
│  directory to hold shard subdirectories&#x27;)                               │
│      args = parser.parse_args()                                              │
│                                                                              │
│      compress(Path(args.input_dir), Path(args.output_dir))                   │
│                                                                              │
│  if __name__ == &#x27;__main__&#x27;:                                        │
│      main()                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ Code: /app/decompress.py                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  #!/usr/bin/env python3                                                      │
│  import argparse                                                             │
│  import json                                                                 │
│  import shutil                                                               │
│  from pathlib import Path                                                    │
│  import hashlib                                                              │
│  from typing import Optional                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│  def sha256_for_path(path: Path, block_size: int = 65536) -&gt; str:         │
│      h = hashlib.sha256()                                                    │
│      with path.open(&#x27;rb&#x27;) as f:                                    │
│          for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(block_size), b&#x27;&#x27;):       │
│              h.update(chunk)                                                 │
│      return h.hexdigest()                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  def decompress(resharded_dir: Path, stage_dir: Optional[Path], verify:      │
│  bool):                                                                      │
│      resharded_dir = resharded_dir.resolve()                                 │
│      if not resharded_dir.exists() or not resharded_dir.is_dir():            │
│          raise SystemExit(f&quot;Resharded dir does not exist or is not a    │
│  directory: {resharded_dir}&quot;)                                           │
│                                                                              │
│      staging_root = stage_dir.resolve() if stage_dir is not None else None   │
│                                                                              │
│      # Collect shards                                                        │
│      shards = sorted([p for p in resharded_dir.iterdir() if p.is_dir() and   │
│  p.name.startswith(&#x27;shard_&#x27;)])                                     │
│      if not shards:                                                          │
│          print(&quot;No shard directories found. Nothing to                  │
│  decompress.&quot;)                                                          │
│          return                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│      total_reconstructed = 0                                                 │
│      total_mismatches = 0                                                    │
│      mismatches = []  # (path, expected, actual)                             │
│      to_move = []      # list of (from_path, final_path)                     │
│                                                                              │
│      for shard_dir in shards:                                                │
│          manifest_path = shard_dir / &#x27;manifest.json&#x27;               │
│          if not manifest_path.exists():                                      │
│              print(f&quot;Warning: shard {shard_dir} missing manifest.json,  │
│  skipping&quot;)                                                             │
│              continue                                                        │
│          with manifest_path.open(&#x27;r&#x27;, encoding=&#x27;utf-8&#x27;)  │
│  as mf:                                                                      │
│              manifest = json.load(mf)                                        │
│          base_dir = manifest.get(&#x27;base_dir&#x27;) if                    │
│  isinstance(manifest, dict) else None                                        │
│          entries = manifest.get(&#x27;entries&#x27;) if                      │
│  isinstance(manifest, dict) else manifest                                    │
│                                                                              │
│          for entry in entries:                                               │
│              internal = shard_dir / entry[&#x27;internal_name&#x27;]         │
│              if not internal.exists():                                       │
│                  raise SystemExit(f&quot;Missing data file {internal} for    │
│  original path {entry[&#x27;original_path&#x27;]}&quot;)                     │
│                                                                              │
│              final_path = Path(base_dir) / entry[&#x27;original_path&#x27;]  │
│  if base_dir else Path(entry[&#x27;original_path&#x27;])                     │
│                                                                              │
│              if staging_root:                                                │
│                  staging_path = staging_root /                               │
│  entry[&#x27;original_path&#x27;]                                            │
│                  staging_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)      │
│                  shutil.copy2(str(internal), str(staging_path))              │
│                                                                              │
│                  if verify:                                                  │
│                      actual_hash = sha256_for_path(staging_path)             │
│                      if actual_hash != entry.get(&#x27;sha256&#x27;):        │
│                          total_mismatches += 1                               │
│                          mismatches.append((str(staging_path),               │
│  entry.get(&#x27;sha256&#x27;), actual_hash))                                │
│                          continue  # skip moving this one                    │
│                  to_move.append((staging_path, final_path))                  │
│              else:                                                           │
│                  final_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)        │
│                  shutil.copy2(str(internal), str(final_path))                │
│                  if verify:                                                  │
│                      actual_hash = sha256_for_path(final_path)               │
│                      if actual_hash != entry.get(&#x27;sha256&#x27;):        │
│                          total_mismatches += 1                               │
│                          mismatches.append((str(final_path),                 │
│  entry.get(&#x27;sha256&#x27;), actual_hash))                                │
│                          continue                                            │
│                  total_reconstructed += 1                                    │
│                                                                              │
│      # Finalize staging moves                                                │
│      if staging_root:                                                        │
│          if verify and total_mismatches:                                     │
│              print(f&quot;Decompression failed: {total_mismatches}           │
│  mismatches detected.&quot;)                                                 │
│              for i, (p, exp, act) in enumerate(mismatches[:5], start=1):     │
│                  print(f&quot;  MISMATCH: {p}  expected={exp}                │
│  actual={act}&quot;)                                                         │
│              raise SystemExit(2)                                             │
│          # Move staged files into final destinations                         │
│          for staged_path, final_path in to_move:                             │
│              final_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)            │
│              if final_path.exists():                                         │
│                  final_path.unlink()                                         │
│              shutil.move(str(staged_path), str(final_path))                  │
│              total_reconstructed += 1                                        │
│          # Cleanup staging area                                              │
│          if staging_root and staging_root.exists():                          │
│              shutil.rmtree(staging_root)                                     │
│                                                                              │
│      print(f&quot;Decompression complete. Reconstructed                      │
│  {total_reconstructed} files.&quot;)                                         │
│      if total_mismatches:                                                    │
│          print(f&quot;Total mismatches: {total_mismatches}&quot;)            │
│          for i, (p, exp, act) in enumerate(mismatches[:10], start=1):        │
│              print(f&quot;  {i}. {p} | expected: {exp} | actual:             │
│  {act}&quot;)                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│  def main():                                                                 │
│      parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=&#x27;Decompress           │
│  resharded data back to original structure (with optional staging and        │
│  verification).&#x27;)                                                       │
│      parser.add_argument(&#x27;resharded_dir&#x27;, help=&#x27;Directory     │
│  containing resharded shard_*/manifest.json&#x27;)                           │
│      parser.add_argument(&#x27;--stage-dir&#x27;,                            │
│  dest=&#x27;stage_dir&#x27;, help=&#x27;Stage decompression here before      │
│  finalizing (safer)&#x27;)                                                   │
│      parser.add_argument(&#x27;--verify&#x27;,                               │
│  action=&#x27;store_true&#x27;, help=&#x27;Verify hashes after               │
│  decompression against manifest&#x27;)                                       │
│      args = parser.parse_args()                                              │
│      decompress(Path(args.resharded_dir), Path(args.stage_dir) if            │
│  args.stage_dir else None, args.verify)                                      │
│                                                                              │
│  if __name__ == &#x27;__main__&#x27;:                                        │
│      main()                                                                  │

│ (--stage-dir) and the verification flag (--verify).\n  - After successful    │
│ in-stage verification, data was finalized into the original target paths.\n  │
│ - Verification was byte-for-byte: reconstructed files in the original target │
│ tree matched the manifest SHA-256s.\n- Confirmed reconstruction results:\n   │

│ (shard_*/manifest.json under /app/reshard_output_hash) exist but are not     │
│ counted toward the reconstructed dataset\n- Also validated that a naive      │
│ directory-wide file count (including shards and manifests) is not used for   │
│ integrity checks; the test targeted the exact original tree.\n\nWhy this is  │
│ correct\n- Byte-for-byte guarantee: each reconstructed file’s content hash   │
│ matches the corresponding hash stored in the shard manifests.\n- Safe        │
│ finalization: staging-based reconstruction prevents partial/inconsistent     │
│ in-place updates during verification.\n- Focused verification on the         │
│ original target tree ensures shard artifacts do not skew                     │
│ results.\n\nGeneralization plan for other slices\n- Implement a minimal      │
│ multi-slice harness to automate the same flow across many slices:\n  - New   │
│ script: /app/reshard_all.py\n  - It will iterate over slice directories,     │
│ read a small config (optional) to determine input_dir and output_dir, then   │
│ run:\n    - python3 /app/compress.py <input_dir> <output_dir>\n    - python3 │
│ /app/decompress.py <output_dir> --stage-dir <slice_dir>/stage --verify\n  -  │
│ After successful per-slice verification, the harness can finalize by moving  │
│ staged data into place (or report ready-to-move state).\n- Practical config  │
│ convention (optional but helpful):\n  - Each slice can include               │
│ /app/slice_X/config.json with:\n    - {"input_dir": "input", "output_dir":   │
│ "reshards"}\n  - The harness can read this to determine paths                │
│ automatically.\n\nMinimal patches I’d apply next (if you want me to          │
│ proceed)\n- Add /app/reshard_all.py to orchestrate multiple slices with a    │
│ safe default layout and an optional --dry-run flag.\n- Extend                │
│ /app/decompress.py to expose the --stage-dir and --verify options in a       │
│ robust, reusable way (already outlined in previous messages).\n- Optionally  │
│ add /app/verify_hashes.py for cross-checks against a baseline                │
│ manifest.\n\nTesting workflow you can run (for multiple slices)\n- For each  │
│ slice:\n  - Compress:\n    - python3 /app/compress.py /path/to/slice/input   │
│ /path/to/slice/reshards\n  - Decompress with staging and verify:\n    -      │
│ python3 /app/decompress.py /path/to/slice/reshards --stage-dir               │
│ /path/to/slice/reconstructed_stage --verify\n  - If verification passes,     │
│ finalize (move from staging into place) or rely on the script to finalize    │

│     • decompression used a staging directory (--stage-dir) and the           │

│                                                                              │

│    the corresponding hash stored in the shard manifests.                     │
│  • Safe finalization: staging-based reconstruction prevents                  │
│    partial/inconsistent in-place updates during verification.                │
│  • Focused verification on the original target tree ensures shard artifacts  │
│    do not skew results.                                                      │

│        • python3 /app/decompress.py <output_dir> --stage-dir                 │

│     • After successful per-slice verification, the harness can finalize by   │

│ finish_reason='stop' content='Concise task summary\n\nObjective\n- Build a   │
│ pair of scripts to reshard a dataset and then revert to the original         │
│ structure with byte-for-byte integrity guarantees.\n- Validate on the        │
│ c4_sample slice and make the approach generalizable to other slices.\n\nWhat │
│ was implemented\n- /app/compress.py\n  - In addition to resharing files into │
│ shards (max 30 files per shard, max 15 MB per file), now computes a SHA-256  │
│ hash for every input file.\n  - Each shard’s manifest.json includes:\n    -  │
│ internal_name\n    - original_path\n    - size\n    - sha256\n-              │
│ /app/decompress.py\n  - Enhanced with:\n    - --stage-dir <path> to          │
│ reconstruct into a staging tree first\n    - --verify to verify per-file     │
│ sha256 against the manifest\n  - Behavior:\n    - If stage-dir is provided:  │
│ reconstruct into stage, verify if requested, then move verified files into   │
│ their final in-place paths derived from the manifest\n    - If stage-dir is  │
│ not provided: reconstruct directly into final paths, with optional           │
│ verification\n  - Outputs a concise summary (reconstructed_count,            │
│ mismatches, and sample path/sha256 checks)\n\nWhat was tested\n- Target:     │
│ /app/c4_sample\n- Compression: produced ~330 shards (for 9898 files, 30 per  │
│ shard) with per-file sha256s in each manifest\n- Decompression: run with     │
│ staging and verification\n  - reconstructed_file_count: 9898\n  -            │
│ verification_status: pass\n  - Shard artifacts remain under                  │
│ /app/reshard_output_hash but are not counted in the reconstructed            │
│ dataset\n\nKey findings and lessons\n- File-count verification is            │
│ insufficient: shard files, manifests, and tooling inflate counts if counted  │
│ at the root.\n- Per-file hashes in manifests provide true byte-for-byte      │
│ verification against the original data.\n- Staging area enables safe         │
│ verification before finalizing in-place reconstruction, reducing risk of     │
│ partial updates.\n- The approach is generic: once the manifests include      │
│ sha256, the same flow can be applied to other slices with similar            │
│ structure.\n\nGeneralization plan (for other slices)\n- Introduce a          │
│ lightweight harness to process multiple slices:\n  - Proposed                │
│ /app/reshard_all.py to iterate slices, use a simple config (optional) to     │
│ locate input_dir and output_dir per slice, and run:\n    - compress.py       │
│ <input_dir> <output_dir>\n    - decompress.py <output_dir> --stage-dir       │
│ <slice_stage_dir> --verify\n- Optional per-slice config (config.json) to     │
│ specify input_dir and output_dir per slice.\n\nNext steps                    │
│ (recommendations)\n- If you want, I can apply:\n  - A minimal                │
│ /app/reshard_all.py harness to automate multi-slice processing\n  - An       │
│ optional verify_hashes.py for cross-checks against a baseline manifest\n-    │
│ Re-run the flow on additional slices and provide per-slice integrity reports │
│ (reconstructed_file_count, verification_status, sample hashes)\n\nConcise    │
│ verdict for c4_sample\n- Solved: yes, with robust integrity verification\n-  │
│ Reconstructed file count: 9898\n- Verification: pass\n- Notes: shard         │
│ artifacts exist but are not included in the final reconstructed              │
│ dataset\n\nIf you’d like, I can proceed with adding the multi-slice harness  │
│ and run it on more slices, returning per-slice integrity reports.'           │

│ Concise task summary                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│ Objective                                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  • Build a pair of scripts to reshard a dataset and then revert to the       │
│    original structure with byte-for-byte integrity guarantees.               │
│  • Validate on the c4_sample slice and make the approach generalizable to    │
│    other slices.                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ What was implemented                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│  • /app/compress.py                                                          │
│     • In addition to resharing files into shards (max 30 files per shard,    │
│       max 15 MB per file), now computes a SHA-256 hash for every input file. │
│     • Each shard’s manifest.json includes:                                   │
│        • internal_name                                                       │
│        • original_path                                                       │
│        • size                                                                │
│        • sha256                                                              │
│  • /app/decompress.py                                                        │
│     • Enhanced with:                                                         │
│        • --stage-dir <path> to reconstruct into a staging tree first         │
│        • --verify to verify per-file sha256 against the manifest             │
│     • Behavior:                                                              │
│        • If stage-dir is provided: reconstruct into stage, verify if         │
│          requested, then move verified files into their final in-place paths │
│          derived from the manifest                                           │
│        • If stage-dir is not provided: reconstruct directly into final       │
│          paths, with optional verification                                   │
│     • Outputs a concise summary (reconstructed_count, mismatches, and sample │
│       path/sha256 checks)                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ What was tested                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  • Target: /app/c4_sample                                                    │
│  • Compression: produced ~330 shards (for 9898 files, 30 per shard) with     │
│    per-file sha256s in each manifest                                         │
│  • Decompression: run with staging and verification                          │
│     • reconstructed_file_count: 9898                                         │
│     • verification_status: pass                                              │
│     • Shard artifacts remain under /app/reshard_output_hash but are not      │
│       counted in the reconstructed dataset                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ Key findings and lessons                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│  • File-count verification is insufficient: shard files, manifests, and      │
│    tooling inflate counts if counted at the root.                            │
│  • Per-file hashes in manifests provide true byte-for-byte verification      │
│    against the original data.                                                │
│  • Staging area enables safe verification before finalizing in-place         │
│    reconstruction, reducing risk of partial updates.                         │
│  • The approach is generic: once the manifests include sha256, the same flow │
│    can be applied to other slices with similar structure.                    │
│                                                                              │
│ Generalization plan (for other slices)                                       │
│                                                                              │
│  • Introduce a lightweight harness to process multiple slices:               │
│     • Proposed /app/reshard_all.py to iterate slices, use a simple config    │
│       (optional) to locate input_dir and output_dir per slice, and run:      │
│        • compress.py <input_dir> <output_dir>                                │
│        • decompress.py <output_dir> --stage-dir <slice_stage_dir> --verify   │
│  • Optional per-slice config (config.json) to specify input_dir and          │
│    output_dir per slice.                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ Next steps (recommendations)                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│  • If you want, I can apply:                                                 │
│     • A minimal /app/reshard_all.py harness to automate multi-slice          │
│       processing                                                             │
│     • An optional verify_hashes.py for cross-checks against a baseline       │
│       manifest                                                               │
│  • Re-run the flow on additional slices and provide per-slice integrity      │
│    reports (reconstructed_file_count, verification_status, sample hashes)    │
│                                                                              │
│ Concise verdict for c4_sample                                                │
│                                                                              │
│  • Solved: yes, with robust integrity verification                           │
│  • Reconstructed file count: 9898                                            │
│  • Verification: pass                                                        │
│  • Notes: shard artifacts exist but are not included in the final            │
│    reconstructed dataset                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ If you’d like, I can proceed with adding the multi-slice harness and run it  │
│ on more slices, returning per-slice integrity reports.                       │
